182→\tv.SetConfigName(\"config\") // search for config.{json,yaml,toml}\n   183→\tv.AddConfigPath(\".\")      // cwd fallback\n   184→\n   185→\t// If path points to a file, use it directly; else [License](#license)\n    26→\n    27→---\n    28→\n    29→## Why EchoPulse?\n    30→* Pure Go codebase → simple cross-compiles, small runtime footprint, first-class concurrency.\n    31→* Pluggable **Strategy Pattern**-based ML pipelines—swap sentiment engines without touching ingestion code.\n    32→* End-to-end **MLOps**: automated data versioning, experiment tracking with OpenTelemetry lineage, model registry & gated promotion.\n    33→* **Event-driven everything**: Apache Kafka or NATS JetStream as the backbone; gRPC for service contracts.\n    34→* **Real-time moderation** & community health scoring at millions of events/sec.\n    35→\n    36→---\n    37→\n    38→## Key Features\n    39→| Category            | Feature                                                                                                                                 |\n    40→|---------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    41→| Ingestion           | High-throughput connectors (WebSocket, HTTP SSE, Discord, Twitch, Slack, X) with dynamic back-pressure.                                 |\n    42→| Feature Store       | Temporal, versioned feature store backed by Redis-RocksDB (for hot paths) + S3 (cold paths).                                             |\n    43→| NLP/ML              | Sentiment, stance, toxicity, trend surfacing, entity linking—each as a decoupled strategy.                                              |\n    44→| MLOps               | Experiment tracking (`mlflow` REST compat), hyper-param search via Optuna, live drift detection triggers.                               |\n    45→| Observability       | Grafana dashboards, OpenTelemetry traces, Prometheus metrics, Loki logs.                                                                 |\n    46→| Extensibility       | Observer + Factory patterns allow user-defined sinks and model strategies in <100 LOC.                                                  |\n    47→| Security            | Mutual-TLS for gRPC, RBAC for API gateways, encryption-at-rest for feature store.                                                       |\n    48→\n    49→---\n    50→\n 82→---\n    83→\n    84→## Core Concepts\n    85→\n    86→### SocialEvent\n    87→```go\n    88→type SocialEvent struct {\n    89→    ID          uuid.UUID      `json:\"id\"`\n    90→    Timestamp   time.Time      `json:\"ts\"`\n    91→    Source      string         `json:\"source\"`   // e.g. \"twitch\"\n    92→    ChannelID   string         `json:\"channel\"`  // room or stream ID\n    93→    UserID      string         `json:\"user\"`\n    94→    RawPayload  json.RawMessage `json:\"raw\"`     // original artifact\n    95→    Metadata    map[string]any `json:\"meta\"`     // language, emotes, etc.\n    96→}\n    97→```\n    98→\n    99→### Pipeline Stages\n   100→1. `Extractor`  → text normalization, entity spotting  \n   101→2. `Analyzer`   → model inference (sentiment, toxicity, etc.)  \n   102→3. `Aggregator` → rolling metrics, conversation clustering  \n   103→4. `Publisher`  → pushes results to dashboards / moderation bots\n   104→\n   105→Each stage is a Go interface whose concrete implementations are loaded via DI container (`pkg/di`).\n   106→\n   107→---\n   108→\n   109→## Quick Start\n   110→\n   111→Prerequisites  \n   112→* Go 1.22  \n   113→* Docker ≥ 24  \n   114→* Make ≥ 4\n   115→\n   116→```bash\n   117→git clone https://github.com/your-org/echopulse\n   118→cd echopulse\n   119→make bootstrap       # installs dev tooling (golangci-lint, mockgen, etc.)\n   120→make compose-up      # spins up Kafka, Redis, Postgres, Jaeger, Grafana\n   121→make run             # starts all Go services\n   122→```\n   123→\n   124→Open **`http://localhost:3000`** (Grafana) and import dashboard `deploy/grafana/echo-pulse.json`.\n   125→\n   126→To tail logs:\n   127→\n   128→```bash\n   129→docker compose logs -f ingestion-gw pipeline-svc\n   130→```\n   131→\n   132→---\n   133→\n   134→## Development Workflow\n   135→\n   136→Task                  | Command\n [Why EchoPulse?](#why-echopulse)\n2. [Key Features](#key-features)\n3. [Architecture Overview](#architecture-overview)\n4. [Core Concepts](#core-concepts)\n5. [Quick Start](#quick-start)\n6. [Contributing](#contributing)\n12. [License](#license)\n\n---\n\n## Why EchoPulse?\n* Pure Go codebase → simple cross-compiles, small runtime footprint, first-class concurrency.\n* Pluggable **Strategy Pattern**-based ML pipelines—swap sentiment engines without touching ingestion code.\n* End-to-end **MLOps**: automated data versioning, experiment tracking with OpenTelemetry lineage, model registry & gated promotion.\n* **Event-driven everything**: Apache Kafka or NATS JetStream as the backbone; gRPC for service contracts.\n* **Real-time moderation** & community health scoring at millions of events/sec.\n\n---\n\n## Key Features\n| Category            | Feature                                                                                                                                 |\n|---------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Ingestion           | High-throughput connectors (WebSocket, HTTP SSE, Discord, Twitch, Slack, X) with dynamic back-pressure.                                 |\n| Feature Store       | Temporal, versioned feature store backed by Redis-RocksDB (for hot paths) + S3 (cold paths).                                             |\n| NLP/ML              | Sentiment, stance, toxicity, trend surfacing, entity linking—each as a decoupled strategy.                                              |\n| MLOps               | Experiment tracking (`mlflow` REST compat), hyper-param search via Optuna, live drift detection triggers.                               |\n| Observability       | Grafana dashboards, OpenTelemetry traces, Prometheus metrics, Loki logs.                                                                 |\n| Extensibility       | Observer + Factory patterns allow user-defined sinks and model strategies in <100 LOC.                                                  |\n| Security            | Mutual-TLS for gRPC, RBAC for API gateways, encryption-at-rest for feature store.                                                       |\n\n---\n\n## Architecture Overview\n```text\n                                     ┌──────────────────────┐\n    ┌─────────Social Platforms──────►│  Ingestion Gateway   │◄───────────Config/ACL\n    │  (chat, tweets, audio)         └─────────┬────────────┘\n    │                                         ︎︎︎⏬\n    │    HTTP/SSE/WebSocket                   Kafka / JetStream\n    │                                           ⬇\n    │    ┌──────────────────────┐   ┌─────────────────────────┐\n    └───►│ Canonical Event Bus  │──►│ Real-Time Pipelines     │─┐\n         └──────────────────────┘   │  ├─ Feature Extractors  │ │\n                                    │  ├─ Sentiment Service   │ │ gRPC\n                                    │  ├─ Toxicity Service    │ │\n                                    │  └─ Trend Surface       │ │\n                                    └─────────────────────────┘ │\n                                          ⬇                    │\n                                    ┌───────────────┐          │\n                                    │ Feature Store │◄─────────┘\n                                    └───────────────┘\n                                           ⬇\n                                    ┌──────────────────────┐\n                                    │ Model Registry (gRPC)│\n                                    └──────────────────────┘\n                                           ⬇\n                                    ┌──────────────────────┐\n                                    │  Visualization API   │\n                                    └──────────────────────┘\n```\n\nAll services implement `pkg/contracts/*.proto` and share a common `internal/bus` package for event publication/subscription.\n\n---\n\n## Core Concepts\n\n### SocialEvent\n```go\ntype SocialEvent struct {\n    ID          uuid.UUID      `json:\"id\"`\n    Timestamp   time.Time      `json:\"ts\"`\n    Source      string         `json:\"source\"`   // e.g. \"twitch\"\n    ChannelID   string         `json:\"channel\"`  // room or stream ID\n    UserID      string         `json:\"user\"`\n    RawPayload  json.RawMessage `json:\"raw\"`     // original artifact\n    Metadata    map[string]any `json:\"meta\"`     // language, emotes, etc.\n}\n```\n\n### Pipeline Stages\n1. `Extractor`  → text normalization, entity spotting  \n2. `Analyzer`   → model inference (sentiment, toxicity, etc.)  \n3. `Aggregator` → rolling metrics, conversation clustering  \n4. `Publisher`  → pushes results to dashboards / moderation bots\n\nEach stage is a Go interface whose concrete implementations are loaded via DI container (`pkg/di`).\n\n---\n\n## Quick Start\n\nPrerequisites  \n* Go 1.22  \n* Docker ≥ 24  \n* Make ≥ 4\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/your-org/echopulse\ncd echopulse\nmake bootstrap       # installs dev tooling (golangci-lint, mockgen, etc.)\nmake compose-up      # spins up Kafka, Redis, Postgres, Jaeger, Grafana\nmake run             # starts all Go services\n```\n\nOpen **`http://localhost:3000`** (Grafana) and import dashboard `deploy/grafana/echo-pulse.json`.\n\nTo tail logs:\n\n```bash\ndocker compose logs -f ingestion-gw pipeline-svc\n```\n\n---\n\n## Development test`\nLint + Vet            | `make lint`\nBenchmark             | `make bench`\nHot Reload Services   | `make dev` (requires [air](https://github.com/cosmtrek/air))\nRelease Binary        | `make build TAG=v1.2.0`\n\n---\n\n## CLI Usage\nEchoPulse ships with a CLI for bootstrapping pipelines and poking the feature store.\n\n```bash\n$ echopulse --help\nReal-Time Social Signal Processing Platform\n\nUsage:\n  echopulse [command]\n\nAvailable Commands:\n  ingest        Start ingestion gateway\n  pipeline      Run pipeline worker pool\n  feature       Query the feature store\n  registry      Interact with model registry\n  completion    Generate shell completion script\n```\n\nExample: export features used by the sentiment model.\n\n```bash\nechopulse feature export \\\n  --model sentiment@sha256:abcd \\\n  --output s3://bucket/dataset.parquet\n```\n\n---\n\n## Event Schemas\n\n### Protobuf (canonical event)\n\n```proto\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\n\npackage contracts;\n\nimport \"google/protobuf/timestamp.proto\";\n\nmessage SocialEvent {\n  string id            = 1;\n  google.protobuf.Timestamp ts = 2;\n  string source        = 3;\n  string channel_id    = 4;\n  string user_id       = 5;\n  bytes raw_payload    = 6;\n  map<string,string> metadata = 7;\n}\n```\n\n### Avro (analysis result)\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"record\",\n  \"name\": \"AnalysisResult\",\n  \"namespace\": \"events\",\n  \"fields\": [\n    {\"name\": \"event_id\",      \"type\": \"string\"},\n    {\"name\": \"sentiment\",     \"type\": \"string\"},\n    {\"name\": \"toxicity\",      \"type\": \"double\"},\n    {\"name\": \"entities\",      \"type\": {\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":\"string\"}},\n    {\"name\": \"ts\",            \"type\": {\"type\":\"long\",\"logicalType\":\"timestamp-millis\"}}\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Extending Pipelines\n\nCreate a new toxicity strategy powered by [Perspective API]:\n\n```go\ntype perspectiveScore struct {\n    Score float64 `json:\"score\"`\n}\n\ntype PerspectiveStrategy struct {\n    endpoint string\n    client   *http.Client\n}\n\nfunc (p *PerspectiveStrategy) Name() string { return \"perspective\" }\n\nfunc (p *PerspectiveStrategy) Analyze(ctx context.Context, in *model.Document) (*model.Toxicity, error) {\n    reqBody, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{\n        \"comment\": map[string]string{\"text\": in.Text},\n        \"languages\": []string{\"en\"},\n        \"requestedAttributes\": map[string]any{\"TOXICITY\": {}},\n    })\n    req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, p.endpoint, bytes.NewReader(reqBody))\n    if err != nil {\n        return nil, err\n    }\n\n    resp, err := p.client.Do(req)\n    if err != nil {\n        return nil, err\n    }\n    defer resp.Body.Close()\n\n    var payload map[string]map[string]map[string]perspectiveScore\n    if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&payload); err != nil {\n        return nil, err\n    }\n    score := payload[\"attributeScores\"][\"TOXICITY\"][\"summaryScore\"].Score\n    return &model.Toxicity{Value: score, Provider: p.Name()}, nil\n}\n\nfunc init() {\n    strategy.RegisterToxicity(&PerspectiveStrategy{\n        endpoint: os.Getenv(\"PERSPECTIVE_ENDPOINT\"),\n        client: &http.Client{\n            Timeout: 2 * time.Second,\n        },\n    })\n}\n```\n\nDrop the file anywhere under `internal/strategies/`, rebuild, and the DI container will auto-discover it.\n\n---\n\n## Observability\n\nEchoPulse instruments every goroutine with OpenTelemetry.  \nBy default, traces are pushed to **Jaeger**:\n\n```bash\n# port-forward if running in k8s\nkubectl port-forward svc/jaeger 16686:16686\nopen http://localhost:16686/search\n```\n\nPrometheus scrapes `/metrics` from each component; dashboards are pre-packed in `deploy/grafana/`.\n\nCritical SLIs exposed:\n* `event_ingest_lag_seconds`\n* `pipeline_proc_duration_seconds`\n* `model_inference_seconds`\n* `drift_alert_total`\n\n---\n\n## Contributing\n\nWe :heart: contributions!\n\n1. 139→\t\t\tclose(ch)\n   140→\t\t\tdelete(b.subs, id)\n   141→\t\t}\n   142→\t\tb.subsMu.Unlock()\n   143→\t}\n   144→\treturn unsub, nil\n   145→}\n   146→\n   147→func (b *inMemoryBus) Shutdown(_ 78→\tcase 0:\n    79→\t\treturn \"\"\n    80→\tcase 1:\n    81→\t\treturn ss[0]\n    82→\tdefault:\n    83→\t\tout := \"\"\n    84→\t\tfor i, s := range ss {\n    85→\t\t\tout += s\n    86→\t\t\tif i   100→\tSubscribe(ctx context.Context, topic string) (<-chan SocialEvent, error)\n   101→}\n   102→\n   103→// EventBusPublisher abstracts producers for HealthScore results.\n   104→type -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   109→// Strategy Pattern for Health Scoring\n   110→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   111→\n   112→// HealthScoreStrategy encapsulates the algorithm that converts aggregated\n   113→// features into a WeightedHeuristicsStrategy is a simple, fast, interpretable implementation\n   120→// of HealthScoreStrategy.  In production we also ship transformer-based models.\n   121→type WeightedHeuristicsStrategy struct {\n   122→\tweights map[string]float64 // feature -> weight\n   123→}\n   124→\n   125→// NewWeightedHeuristicsStrategy returns an opinionated default.\n   126→func NewWeightedHeuristicsStrategy() *WeightedHeuristicsStrategy {\n   127→\treturn &WeightedHeuristicsStrategy{\n   128→\t\tweights: map[string]float64{\n   129→\t\t\t\"sentiment.pos\":  +0.6,\n   130→\t\t\t\"sentiment.neg\":  -0.8,\n   131→\t\t\t\"toxicity\":       -1.0,\n   132→\t\t\t\"engagement\":     +0.3,\n   133→\t\t\t\"stance.polar\":   -0.5,\n   134→\t\t\t\"wellness\":       +0.4,\n   135→\t\t\t\"question_ratio\": +0.2,\n   136→\t\t},\n   137→\t}\n   138→}\n   139→\n   140→// Version returns a semantic 144→// NOTE: It is intentionally simple yet non-trivial for demo purposes.\n   145→func (w *WeightedHeuristicsStrategy) Compute(window []SocialEvent) (float64, error) {\n   146→\tif len(window) == 0 {\n   147→\t\treturn math.NaN(), errors.New(\"empty window\")\n   148→\t}\n   149→\n   150→\tvar acc, totalWeight float64\n   151→\tfor _, evt := range window {\n   152→\t\tfor k, weight := range math.Abs(weight)\n   157→\t\t\t\t}\n   158→\t\t\t}\n   159→\t\t}\n   160→\t}\n   161→\n   162→\tif totalWeight == 0 {\n   163→\t\treturn 0, nil\n   164→\t}\n   165→\t// Normalize to [-1, 1] for interpretability.\n   166→\treturn clamp(acc/totalWeight, -1.0, 1.0), nil\n   167→}\n   168→\n   169→func castFloat(v any) (float64, bool) {\n   170→\tswitch t := v.(type) {\n   171→\tcase float64:\n   172→\t\treturn t, true\n   173→\tcase float32:\n   174→\t\treturn float64(t), true\n   175→\tcase int64:\n   176→\t\treturn float64(t), true\n   177→\tcase int32:\n   178→\t\treturn float64(t), true\n   179→\tcase int:\n   180→\t\treturn float64(t), true\n   181→\tdefault:\n   182→\t\treturn 0, false\n   183→\t}\n   184→}\n   185→\n   186→func clamp(val, min, max float64) float64 {\n   187→\tif val < min {\n   188→\t\treturn min\n   189→\t}\n   190→\tif val > max {\n   191→\t\treturn max\n   192→\t}\n   193→\treturn val\n   194→}\n   195→\n   196→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   197→// Windowed Event Aggregator\n   198→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   199→\n   200→// windowBucket holds events that fall into the bucket's time-range.\n   201→type windowBucket struct {\n   202→\tfrom time.Time\n   203→\tto   time.Time\n   204→\tevts []SocialEvent\n   205→}\n   206→\n   207→// SlidingWindowAggregator collects events into overlapping windows that\n   208→// advance by `slide` every `slide` duration until a full window of\n   209→// `size` has been accumulated.\n   210→type SlidingWindowAggregator struct {\n   211→\tsize, slide time.Duration\n   212→\tbuckets     []windowBucket\n   213→\tmtx         sync.Mutex\n   214→}\n   215→\n   216→func NewSlidingWindowAggregator(size, slide time.Duration) *SlidingWindowAggregator {\n   217→\treturn &SlidingWindowAggregator{\n   218→\t\tsize:    size,\n   219→\t\tslide:   slide,\n   220→\t\tbuckets: make([]windowBucket, 0, int(size/slide)+1),\n   221→\t}\n   222→}\n   223→\n   224→// addEvent inserts the event into all overlapping buckets.\n   225→func (agg *SlidingWindowAggregator) addEvent(evt SocialEvent) {\n   226→\tagg.mtx.Lock()\n   227→\tdefer agg.mtx.Unlock()\n   228→\n   229→\t// Remove expired buckets.\n   230→\tnow := evt.CreatedAtUTC\n   231→\tcutoff := now.Add(-agg.size)\n   232→\tidx := 0\n   233→\tfor idx < len(agg.buckets) && agg.buckets[idx].to.Before(cutoff) {\n   234→\t\tidx++\n   235→\t}\n   236→\tagg.buckets = agg.buckets[idx:]\n   237→\n   238→\t// Ensure buckets up to current time exist.\n   239→\tlatest := agg.latestBucketEnd()\n   240→\tfor latest.Before(now) {\n   241→\t\tstart := latest\n   242→\t\tend := start.Add(agg.slide)\n   243→\t\tagg.buckets = append(agg.buckets, windowBucket{from: start, to: end})\n   244→\t\tlatest = end\n   245→\t}\n   246→\n   247→\t// Insert event into relevant buckets.\n   248→\tfor i := range agg.buckets {\n   249→\t\tif evt.CreatedAtUTC.After(agg.buckets[i].from) && !evt.CreatedAtUTC.After(agg.buckets[i].to) {\n   250→\t\t\tagg.buckets[i].evts = append(agg.buckets[i].evts, evt)\n   251→\t\t}\n   252→\t}\n   253→}\n   254→\n   255→func (agg *SlidingWindowAggregator) latestBucketEnd() time.Time {\n   256→\tif len(agg.buckets) == 0 {\n   257→\t\treturn time.Now().UTC().Truncate(agg.slide)\n   258→\t}\n   259→\treturn agg.buckets[len(agg.buckets)-1].to\n   260→}\n   261→\n   262→// popWindows returns and deletes buckets whose end‐time <= now ‑ slide.\n   263→func (agg *SlidingWindowAggregator) popWindows(now time.Time) []windowBucket {\n   264→\tagg.mtx.Lock()\n   265→\tdefer agg.mtx.Unlock()\n   266→\n   267→\tvar ready []windowBucket\n   268→\tcut := 0\n   269→\tfor ; cut < len(agg.buckets); cut++ {\n   270→\t\tif agg.buckets[cut].to.After(now.Add(-agg.slide)) {\n   271→\t\t\tbreak\n   272→\t\t}\n   273→\t\tready = append(ready, agg.buckets[cut])\n   274→\t}\n   275→\tagg.buckets = agg.buckets[cut:]\n   276→\treturn ready\n   277→}\n   278→\n   279→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   280→// Community Health Scorer (Observer + Pipeline)\n   281→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   282→\n   283→// CommunityScorer consumes SocialEvents, aggregates them in real-time,\n   284→// 350→\t\tmetrics:    metrics,\n   351→\t}, nil\n   352→}\n   353→\n   354→// Run wires up subscriptions and blocks until ctx is cancelled.\n   355→// It spawns two goroutines:\n   356→//   1. eventLoop:   consumes incoming SocialEvents\n   357→//   2. windowLoop:  periodically evaluates completed windows\n   358→func (s *CommunityScorer) Run(ctx context.Context, topicIn, topicOut string) error {\n   359→\tevtCh, err := s.sub.Subscribe(ctx, topicIn)\n   360→\tif err != nil {\n   361→\t\treturn err\n   362→\t}\n   363→\n   364→\t// Using errgroup ensures both goroutines finish cleanly.\n   365→\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n   366→\twg.Add(2)\n   367→\n   368→\tgo func() {\n   369→\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n   370→\t\ts.eventLoop(ctx, evtCh)\n   371→\t}()\n   372→\n   373→\tgo func() {\n   374→\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n   375→\t\ts.windowLoop(ctx, topicOut)\n   376→\t}()\n   377→\n   378→\t<-ctx.Done()\n   379→\tdoneCh := make(chan struct{})\n   380→\tgo func() {\n   381→\t\twg.Wait()\n   382→\t\tclose(doneCh)\n   383→\t}()\n   384→\n   385→\tselect {\n   386→\tcase <-doneCh:\n   387→\tcase <-time.After(s.cfg.GracefulShutdownTimeout):\n   388→\t}\n   389→\n   390→\t// publish remaining partial windows if configured\n   391→\tif s.cfg.PublishPartialWindows {\n   392→\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n   393→\t\twindows :=  399→\treturn nil\n   400→}\n   401→\n   402→// eventLoop ingests events and feeds them to the aggregator.\n   403→func (s *CommunityScorer) eventLoop(ctx context.Context, evtCh <-chan SocialEvent) {\n   404→\tfor {\n   405→\t\tselect {\n   406→\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n   407→\t\t\treturn\n   408→\t\tcase evt, ok := <-evtCh:\n   409→\t\t\tif !ok {\n   410→\t\t\t\treturn\n   411→\t\t\t}\n   412→\t\t\ts.metrics.incomingEvents.Inc()\n   413→\t\t\ts.aggregator.addEvent(evt)\n   414→\t\t}\n   415→\t}\n   416→}\n   417→\n   418→// windowLoop periodically asks the aggregator for completed windows.\n   419→func (s *CommunityScorer) windowLoop(ctx context.Context, topicOut string) {\n   420→\tticker := time.NewTicker(s.cfg.WindowSlide)\n   421→\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n   422→\n   423→\tfor 434→\t\t\t\ts.metrics.windowProcessingTime.Observe(time.Since(start).Seconds())\n   435→\t\t\t}\n   436→\t\t}\n   437→\t}\n   438→}\n   439→\n   440→// processWindow applies the strategy and publishes the score.\n   441→func (s *CommunityScorer) processWindow(ctx context.Context, topicOut string, events []SocialEvent) error {\n   442→\tscoreVal, err := s.strategy.Compute(events)\n   443→\tif err != 450→\t\tperChannel[e.ChannelID] = append(perChannel[e.ChannelID], e)\n   451→\t}\n   452→\n   453→\tfor chID, evts := range perChannel {\n   454→\t\tscoreVal, err = s.strategy.Compute(evts)\n   455→\t\tif err != nil {\n   456→\t\t\treturn err\n   457→\t\t}\n   458→\t\tscore := HealthScore{\n   459→\t\t\tChannelID: chID,\n   460→\t\t\tScore:     scoreVal,\n   461→\t\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n   462→\t\t\tVersion:   s.strategy.Version(),\n   463→\t\t}\n   464→\t\tif err := s.pub.Publish(ctx, topicOut, score); err != nil {\n   465→\t\t\treturn err\n   466→\t\t}\n   467→\t\ts.metrics.publishedScores.Inc()\n   468→\t}\n   469→\treturn nil\n   470→}\n   471→\n   472→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   473→// len(window) == 0 {\n\t\treturn math.NaN(), errors.New(\"empty window\")\n\t}\n\n\tvar acc, totalWeight float64\n\tfor _, evt := range window {\n\t\tfor k, weight := range w.weights {\n\t\t\tif vAny, ok := evt.Extracted[k]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tif v, ok := castFloat(vAny); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tacc += v * weight\n\t\t\t\t\ttotalWeight += math.Abs(weight)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif totalWeight == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\t// Normalize to [-1, 1] for interpretability.\n\treturn clamp(acc/totalWeight, -1.0, 1.0), nil\n}\n\nfunc castFloat(v any) (float64, bool) {\n\tswitch t := v.(type) {\n\tcase float64:\n\t\treturn t, true\n\tcase float32:\n\t\treturn float64(t), true\n\tcase int64:\n\t\treturn float64(t), true\n\tcase int32:\n\t\treturn float64(t), true\n\tcase int:\n\t\treturn float64(t), true\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn 0, false\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc clamp(val, min, max float64) float64 {\n\tif val < min {\n\t\treturn min\n\t}\n\tif val > max {\n\t\treturn max\n\t}\n\treturn val\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Windowed Event Aggregator\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// windowBucket holds events that fall into the bucket's time-range.\ntype windowBucket struct {\n\tfrom time.Time\n\tto   time.Time\n\tevts []SocialEvent\n}\n\n// SlidingWindowAggregator collects events into overlapping windows that\n// advance by `slide` every `slide` duration until a full window of\n// `size` has been accumulated.\ntype SlidingWindowAggregator struct {\n\tsize, slide time.Duration\n\tbuckets     []windowBucket\n\tmtx         sync.Mutex\n}\n\nfunc NewSlidingWindowAggregator(size, slide time.Duration) *SlidingWindowAggregator {\n\treturn &SlidingWindowAggregator{\n\t\tsize:    size,\n\t\tslide:   slide,\n\t\tbuckets: make([]windowBucket, 0, int(size/slide)+1),\n\t}\n}\n\n// addEvent inserts the event into all overlapping buckets.\nfunc (agg *SlidingWindowAggregator) addEvent(evt SocialEvent) {\n\tagg.mtx.Lock()\n\tdefer agg.mtx.Unlock()\n\n\t// Remove expired buckets.\n\tnow := evt.CreatedAtUTC\n\tcutoff := now.Add(-agg.size)\n\tidx := 0\n\tfor idx < len(agg.buckets) && agg.buckets[idx].to.Before(cutoff) {\n\t\tidx++\n\t}\n\tagg.buckets = agg.buckets[idx:]\n\n\t// Ensure buckets up to current time exist.\n\tlatest := agg.latestBucketEnd()\n\tfor latest.Before(now) {\n\t\tstart := latest\n\t\tend := start.Add(agg.slide)\n\t\tagg.buckets = append(agg.buckets, windowBucket{from: start, to: end})\n\t\tlatest = end\n\t}\n\n\t// Insert event into relevant buckets.\n\tfor i := range agg.buckets {\n\t\tif evt.CreatedAtUTC.After(agg.buckets[i].from) && !evt.CreatedAtUTC.After(agg.buckets[i].to) {\n\t\t\tagg.buckets[i].evts = append(agg.buckets[i].evts, evt)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (agg *SlidingWindowAggregator) latestBucketEnd() time.Time {\n\tif len(agg.buckets) == 0 {\n\t\treturn time.Now().UTC().Truncate(agg.slide)\n\t}\n\treturn agg.buckets[len(agg.buckets)-1].to\n}\n\n// popWindows returns and deletes buckets whose end‐time <= now ‑ slide.\nfunc (agg *SlidingWindowAggregator) popWindows(now time.Time) []windowBucket {\n\tagg.mtx.Lock()\n\tdefer agg.mtx.Unlock()\n\n\tvar ready []windowBucket\n\tcut := 0\n\tfor ; cut < len(agg.buckets); cut++ {\n\t\tif agg.buckets[cut].to.After(now.Add(-agg.slide)) {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tready = append(ready, agg.buckets[cut])\n\t}\n\tagg.buckets = agg.buckets[cut:]\n\treturn ready\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Community Health Scorer (Observer + Pipeline)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// CommunityScorer consumes SocialEvents, aggregates them in real-time,\n// calculates community-health scores, and publishes HealthScore events.\ntype CommunityScorer struct {\n\tcfg        ScorerConfig\n\tstrategy   HealthScoreStrategy\n\tsub        EventBusSubscriber\n\tpub        EventBusPublisher\n\taggregator *SlidingWindowAggregator\n\tmetrics    scorerMetrics\n}\n\n// scorerMetrics exposes internal metrics for Prometheus scraping.\ntype scorerMetrics struct {\n\tincomingEvents       prometheus.Counter\n\tpublishedScores      prometheus.Counter\n\tstrategyFailures     prometheus.Counter\n\twindowProcessingTime prometheus.Histogram\n}\n\nfunc newScorerMetrics() scorerMetrics {\n\treturn scorerMetrics{\n\t\tincomingEvents: prometheus.NewCounter(prometheus.CounterOpts{\n\t\t\tName: \"echopulse_scorer_incoming_events_total\",\n\t\t\tHelp: \"Total number of social events ingested by CommunityScorer.\",\n\t\t}),\n\t\tpublishedScores: prometheus.NewCounter(prometheus.CounterOpts{\n\t\t\tName: \"echopulse_scorer_published_scores_total\",\n\t\t\tHelp: \"Total number of health scores published by CommunityScorer.\",\n\t\t}),\n\t\tstrategyFailures: prometheus.NewCounter(prometheus.CounterOpts{\n\t\t\tName: \"echopulse_scorer_strategy_failures_total\",\n\t\t\tHelp: \"Total number of strategy compute failures.\",\n\t\t}),\n\t\twindowProcessingTime: prometheus.NewHistogram(prometheus.HistogramOpts{\n\t\t\tName:    \"echopulse_scorer_window_processing_seconds\",\n\t\t\tHelp:    \"Time it takes to process one window of events.\",\n\t\t\tBuckets: prometheus.ExponentialBuckets(0.0005, 2, 15),\n\t\t}),\n\t}\n}\n\n// Register all metrics to the provided registry; caller decides global vs local.\nfunc (m *scorerMetrics) Register(reg *prometheus.Registry) {\n\treg.MustRegister(\n\t\tm.incomingEvents,\n\t\tm.publishedScores,\n\t\tm.strategyFailures,\n\t\tm.windowProcessingTime,\n\t)\n}\n\n// NewCommunityScorer constructs a scorer with a default heuristic strategy.\nfunc NewCommunityScorer(cfg ScorerConfig, sub EventBusSubscriber, pub EventBusPublisher, reg *prometheus.Registry) (*CommunityScorer, error) {\n\tif err := cfg.validate(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tmetrics := newScorerMetrics()\n\tif reg != nil {\n\t\tmetrics.Register(reg)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &CommunityScorer{\n\t\tcfg:        cfg,\n\t\tstrategy:   NewWeightedHeuristicsStrategy(),\n\t\tsub:        sub,\n\t\tpub:        pub,\n\t\taggregator: NewSlidingWindowAggregator(cfg.WindowSize, cfg.WindowSlide),\n\t\tmetrics:    metrics,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// Run wires up subscriptions and blocks until ctx is cancelled.\n// It spawns two goroutines:\n//   1. eventLoop:   consumes incoming SocialEvents\n//   2. windowLoop:  periodically evaluates completed windows\nfunc (s *CommunityScorer) Run(ctx context.Context, topicIn, topicOut string) error {\n\tevtCh, err := s.sub.Subscribe(ctx, topicIn)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Using errgroup ensures both goroutines finish cleanly.\n\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n\twg.Add(2)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n\t\ts.eventLoop(ctx, evtCh)\n\t}()\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n\t\ts.windowLoop(ctx, topicOut)\n\t}()\n\n\t<-ctx.Done()\n\tdoneCh := make(chan struct{})\n\tgo func() {\n\t\twg.Wait()\n\t\tclose(doneCh)\n\t}()\n\n\tselect {\n\tcase <-doneCh:\n\tcase <-time.After(s.cfg.GracefulShutdownTimeout):\n\t}\n\n\t// publish remaining partial windows if configured\n\tif s.cfg.PublishPartialWindows {\n\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n\t\twindows := s.aggregator.popWindows(now.Add(s.cfg.WindowSize))\n\t\tfor _, w := range windows {\n\t\t\t_ = s.processWindow(ctx, topicOut, w.evts) // best effort\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// eventLoop ingests events and feeds them to the aggregator.\nfunc (s *CommunityScorer) eventLoop(ctx context.Context, evtCh <-chan SocialEvent) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcase evt, ok := <-evtCh:\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ts.metrics.incomingEvents.Inc()\n\t\t\ts.aggregator.addEvent(evt)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// windowLoop periodically asks the aggregator for completed windows.\nfunc (s *CommunityScorer) windowLoop(ctx context.Context, topicOut string) {\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(s.cfg.WindowSlide)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcase ts := <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\twindows := s.aggregator.popWindows(ts)\n\t\t\tfor _, w := range windows {\n\t\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\t\t\t\tif err := s.processWindow(ctx, topicOut, w.evts); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\ts.metrics.strategyFailures.Inc()\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\ts.metrics.windowProcessingTime.Observe(time.Since(start).Seconds())\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// processWindow applies the strategy and publishes the score.\nfunc (s *CommunityScorer) processWindow(ctx context.Context, topicOut string, events []SocialEvent) error {\n\tscoreVal, err := s.strategy.Compute(events)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// We create one HealthScore per channel.\n\tperChannel := make(map[string][]SocialEvent)\n\tfor _, e := range events {\n\t\tperChannel[e.ChannelID] = append(perChannel[e.ChannelID], e)\n\t}\n\n\tfor chID, evts := range perChannel {\n\t\tscoreVal, err = s.strategy.Compute(evts)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\tscore := HealthScore{\n\t\t\tChannelID: chID,\n\t\t\tScore:     scoreVal,\n\t\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\t\tVersion:   s.strategy.Version(),\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := s.pub.Publish(ctx, topicOut, score); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t\ts.metrics.publishedScores.Inc()\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Compile-time interface assertions (a popular Go idiom).\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nvar (\n\t_ HealthScoreStrategy = (*WeightedHeuristicsStrategy)(nil)\n)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Utility\n// File: src/module_69.go\n//\n// EchoPulse – Real-Time Social Signal Processing Platform\n//\n// Sliding-window sentiment aggregation and community-health signalling\n// -------------------------------------------------------------------\n// This module consumes canonical SocialEvents, analyzes their sentiment\n// by means of a pluggable strategy, aggregates scores inside sliding\n// time-windows and publishes CommunityHealthEvents whenever the rolling\n// sentiment of a community breaches a configurable threshold.\n//\n// Architectural patterns used here:\n//   • Strategy          – SentimentAnalyzer interchangeable at runtime\n//   • Factory           – NewSentimentAnalyzer articulates creation logic\n//   • Observer/Pipeline – Aggregator observes SocialEvents and emits\n//                         derived events further down the processing\n//                         pipeline.\n//\n// NOTE: external integrations such as Kafka/NATS are abstracted behind\n//       a Publisher interface to stay transport-agnostic.\n\npackage echopulse\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"math\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/google/uuid\"\n\t\"github.com/prometheus/client_golang/prometheus\"\n)\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Domain types\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// SocialEvent is the canonical representation of a single user activity\n// already normalised by upstream ingestion services.\ntype SocialEvent struct {\n\tID          string    // globally unique event id\n\tCommunityID string    // e.g. Slack workspace, Twitch channel, Mastodon instance\n\tUserID      string    // anonymised user identifier\n\tLanguage    string    // iso-639-1 code\n\tPayload     string    // UTF-8 a community crosses the alert/ok thresholds.\ntype CommunityHealthEvent struct {\n\tID          string    // event id\n\tCommunityID string\n\tTimestamp   time.Time\n\t// Rolling average sentiment inside the last window\n\tMeanSentiment float64\n\t// True if health is degraded (mean sentiment below threshold)\n\tDegraded bool\n\tWindow    time.Duration\n\tReason    string\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Strategy: Sentiment Analyzer\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// SentimentAnalyzer scores arbitrary text in a given language.\ntype SentimentAnalyzer interface {\n\t// Analyze should return a polarity in the range [-1, 1].\n\tAnalyze(text, lang string) (float64, error)\n\t// Name is used for metrics / debugging.\n\tName() string\n}\n\n// NewSentimentAnalyzer is a factory method returning a concrete analyzer by\n// name.  Additional options may configure external APIs, credentials, etc.\nfunc NewSentimentAnalyzer(name string, opts ...AnalyzerOption) (SentimentAnalyzer, error) {\n\tcfg := analyzerConfig{}\n\tfor _, opt := range opts {\n\t\topt(&cfg)\n\t}\n\n\tswitch strings.ToLower(name) {\n\tcase \"\", \"vader\":\n\t\treturn newVADERAnalyzer(cfg), nil\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"analyzer %q not registered\", name)\n\t}\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Analyzer Options / configuration private to the strategy implementation\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntype analyzerConfig struct {\n\tlexiconPath string\n}\n\ntype AnalyzerOption func(*analyzerConfig)\n\n// WithLexicon allows callers to override the default lexicon resource path.\nfunc WithLexicon(path string) AnalyzerOption {\n\treturn func(c *analyzerConfig) { c.lexiconPath = path }\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Very-lightweight VADER port (stub)\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// vaderAnalyzer is a skeletal, offline analyzer that uses a rudimentary\n// sentiment lexicon.  Production builds swap this out for a full-fledged\n// implementation or an on-line model.\ntype vaderAnalyzer struct {\n\tlexicon map[string]float64\n\tname    string\n}\n\nfunc newVADERAnalyzer(cfg analyzerConfig) *vaderAnalyzer {\n\ta := &vaderAnalyzer{\n\t\tlexicon: defaultLexicon(),\n\t\tname:    \"vader-mini\",\n\t}\n\n\t// In production we would load custom lexicon from cfg.lexiconPath here.\n\treturn a\n}\n\nfunc (v *vaderAnalyzer) Name() string { return v.name }\n\nfunc (v *vaderAnalyzer) Analyze(text, lang string) (float64, error) {\n\tif lang != \"\" && !strings.HasPrefix(lang, \"en\") {\n\t\t// naïve language gating; real code would route to language-specific\n\t\t// models.  Neutral assumption for unknown languages.\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\tif text == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar (\n\t\ttokens = tokenize(text)\n\t\tsum    float64\n\t\tcount  int\n\t)\n\n\tfor _, t := range tokens {\n\t\tif val, ok := v.lexicon[strings.ToLower(t)]; ok {\n\t\t\tsum += val\n\t\t\tcount++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif count == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Clamp result to [-1, 1]\n\tscore := sum / float64(count)\n\tif score > 1 {\n\t\tscore = 1\n\t}\n\tif score < -1 {\n\t\tscore = -1\n\t}\n\treturn score, nil\n}\n\n// defaultLexicon provides a minimal hard-coded lexicon to keep the example\n// fully self-contained.\nfunc defaultLexicon() map[string]float64 {\n\treturn map[string]float64{\n\t\t\"love\":  1.0,\n\t\t\"like\":  0.7,\n\t\t\"good\":  0.6,\n\t\t\"nice\":  0.6,\n\t\t\"great\": 0.8,\n\t\t\"lol\":   0.4,\n\n\t\t\"hate\":   -1.0,\n\t\t\"bad\":    -0.6,\n\t\t\"awful\":  -0.8,\n\t\t\"angry\":  -0.7,\n\t\t\"terrible\": -1.0,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc tokenize(text string) []string {\n\treturn strings.FieldsFunc(text, func(r rune) bool { return r == ' ' || r == ',' || r == '.' || r == '!' || r == '?' })\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Publisher abstraction – hides Kafka/NATS/etc. behind an interface\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Publisher pushes events onto a message bus.\ntype Publisher interface {\n\tPublish(ctx context.Context, key string, v any) error\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Sliding window sentiment aggregator\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// AggregatorConfig enumerates runtime configuration knobs.\ntype AggregatorConfig struct {\n\tWindowSize    time.Duration // logical size of the rolling window\n\tFlushInterval time.Duration // background flush cadence\n\tAlertThresh   float64       // mean sentiment below → degraded\n\tAnalyzerName  string        // which sentiment strategy to use\n\tInputBuf      int           // channel buffer\n}\n\n// Validate returns an error on invalid configurations.\nfunc (c AggregatorConfig) Validate() error {\n\tswitch {\n\tcase c.WindowSize <= 0:\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"window size must be positive\")\n\tcase c.FlushInterval <= 0:\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"flush interval must be positive\")\n\tcase c.InputBuf < 0:\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"input buffer negative\")\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// SlidingWindowAggregator implements the Observer role: it consumes\n// SocialEvents, analyzes and aggregates them, then publishes high-level\n// CommunityHealthEvents to the rest of the system.\n//\n// Thread-safe; supports hot-shutdown via context cancellation.\ntype SlidingWindowAggregator of health alerts emitted\",\n\t\t},\n\t\t[]string{\"community\"},\n\t)\n)\n\n// NewSlidingWindowAggregator returns a fully initialised aggregator.\nfunc NewSlidingWindowAggregator(cfg AggregatorConfig, publisher Publisher) (*SlidingWindowAggregator, error) {\n\tif err := cfg.Validate(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tanalyzer, err := NewSentimentAnalyzer(cfg.AnalyzerName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\n\tagg := &SlidingWindowAggregator{\n\t\tcfg:   cancel,\n\t}\n\n\t// Register prometheus collectors. *SlidingWindowAggregator) PublishEvent(evt SocialEvent) {\n\tselect {\n\tcase a.input <- evt:\n\tcase <-a.ctx.Done():\n\t\t// aggregator shutting down – drop event\n\t}\n}\n\n// Shutdown gracefully shuts down all background goroutines.\nfunc (a *SlidingWindowAggregator) Shutdown(ctx context.Context) error {\n\ta.cancel()\n\n\tch := make(chan struct{})\n\tgo func() {\n\t\ta.wg.Wait()\n\t\tclose(ch)\n\t}()\n\n\tselect {\n\tcase <-ch:\n\t\treturn nil\n\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\treturn ctx.Err()\n\t}\n}\n\n// runProcessingLoop is event-driven: it consumes SocialEvents, scores them,\n// and updates rolling stats.\nfunc (a *SlidingWindowAggregator) runProcessingLoop() {\n\tdefer a.wg.Done()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase evt := <-a.input:\n\t\t\tscore, err := a.analyzer.Analyze(evt.Payload, evt.Language)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t// log and skip – for brevity we print to stderr.\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"sentiment analyze error: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ta.ingestScore(evt, score)\n\n\t\tcase <-a.ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// ingestScore mutates aggregate stats in a concurrency-safe manner.\nfunc (a *SlidingWindowAggregator) ingestScore(evt SocialEvent, score float64) {\n\ta.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer a.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tcs, ok := a.rolling[evt.CommunityID]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\tcs = &communitySentiment{}\n\t\ta.rolling[evt.CommunityID] = cs\n\t}\n\n\tcs.sum += score\n\tcs.count++\n\n\tmetricTotalEvents.WithLabelValues(evt.CommunityID).Inc()\n}\n\n// runFlushLoop periodically computes window means and publishes\n// CommunityHealthEvents if thresholds are crossed.\nfunc (a *SlidingWindowAggregator) runFlushLoop() {\n\tdefer a.wg.Done()\n\n\tticker := flushWindow resets the rolling stats atomically and handles signalling.\nfunc (a *SlidingWindowAggregator) flushWindow() {\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\n\ta.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer a.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tfor community, cs := range a.rolling {\n\t\tif cs.count == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tmean := cs.sum / float64(cs.count)\n\t\tmetricMeanSentiment.WithLabelValues(community).Set(mean)\n\n\t\t// community,\n\t\t\tTimestamp:     now,\n\t\t\tMeanSentiment: mean,\n\t\t\tDegraded:      degraded,\n\t\t\tWindow:        a.cfg.WindowSize,\n\t\t\tReason:        fmt.Sprintf(\"rolling_mean=%0.3f threshold=%0.3f\", mean, a.cfg.AlertThresh),\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif degraded {\n\t\t\tmetricHealthAlerts.WithLabelValues(community).Inc()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// We intentionally ignore publish errors because they can be transient,\n\t\t// but production code should have proper retry/backoff strategies.\n\t\tgo func(e CommunityHealthEvent) {\n\t\t\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// In-memory publisher for tests / local runs\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// MemoryPublisher is a thread-safe, bounded queue publisher useful for tests.\ntype MemoryPublisher struct {\n\tmu    sync.Mutex\n\tqueue []any\n}\n\nfunc NewMemoryPublisher() *MemoryPublisher { return &MemoryPublisher{} }\n\nfunc (m *MemoryPublisher) Publish(_ context.Context, _ string, v any) error {\n\tm.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer m.mu.Unlock()\n\tm.queue = append(m.queue, v)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Drain returns and clears the internal queue.\nfunc (m *MemoryPublisher) Drain() []any {\n\tm.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer m.mu.Unlock()\n\tout := make([]any, len(m.queue))\n\tcopy(out, m.queue)\n\tm.queue = m.queue[:0]\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// m.cfg.GroupID)\n\t\tm.wg.Add(1)\n\t\tgo m.loop()\n\t})\n}\n\n// Stop gracefully shuts down the monitor and waits for inflight\n// processing to finish (bounded by Context deadline/cancel).\nfunc (m *DriftMonitor) Stop() {\n\tm.stopOnce.Do(func() {\n\t\tlogrus.Info(\"driftmonitor: stopping\")\n\t\tm.cancel()\n\t\tm.wg.Wait()\n\t\t_ = m.reader.Close()\n\t})\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Event loop\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunc (m *DriftMonitor) loop() {\n\tdefer m.wg.Done()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tmsg, err := m.reader.ReadMessage(m.ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {\n\t\t\t\treturn // normal shutdown\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlogrus.WithError(err).Warn(\"driftmonitor: read error\")\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar metric DriftMetric\n\t\tif err = json.Unmarshal(msg.Value, &metric); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogrus.WithError(err).Warn(\"driftmonitor: invalid json payload\")\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Sanity guard\n\t\tif metric.ModelID == \"\" || metric.Feature == \"\" {\n\t\t\tlogrus.Warn(\"driftmonitor: missing model/feature identifier\")\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Evaluate drift\n\t\tif metric.PSI >= m.cfg.PSIThreshold {\n\t\t\tev := DriftEvent{\n\t\t\t\tModelID: metric.ModelID,\n\t\t\t\tFeature: metric.Feature,\n\t\t\t\tPSI:     metric.PSI,\n\t\t\t\tTime:    metric.Timestamp,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\t\"model\":   ev.ModelID,\n\t\t\t\t\"feature\": ev.Feature,\n\t\t\t\t\"psi\":     fmt.Sprintf(\"%.4f\", ev.PSI),\n\t\t\t}).Warn(\"driftmonitor: drift detected\")\n\n\t\t\t// Forward to downstream handler\n\t\t\tif err := m.handler.OnDrift(m.ctx, ev); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlogrus.WithError(err).Error(\"driftmonitor: handler error\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Default Handler — HTTP trainer client\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// TrainerClient is a minimal REST client that wraps the model-trainer\n// service. In production this might be gRPC, but HTTP keeps the sample\n// self-contained.\ntype TrainerClient interface {\n\t// TriggerRetrain requests an immediate (ad-hoc) training job and\n\t// returns the job identifier on success.\n\tTriggerRetrain(ctx context.Context, modelID, reason string) (string, error)\n}\n\n// HTTPTrainerClient is a best-effort implementation backed by Go's\n// stdlib HTTP stack.\ntype HTTPTrainerClient struct {\n\tEndpoint   string            // e.g. http://trainer.svc.cluster.local:8080\n\tHTTPClient *http.Client      // injected for testability\n\tHeaders    map[string]string // optional static headers (auth, trace, …)\n}\n\n// TriggerRetrain fires a POST /v1/train endpoint with a JSON body.\nfunc (c *HTTPTrainerClient) TriggerRetrain(ctx context.Context, modelID, reason string) (string, error) {\n\tif c.Endpoint == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", errors.New(\"trainerclient: empty endpoint\")\n\t}\n\tbody := map[string]string{\n\t\t\"model_id\": modelID,\n\t\t\"reason\":   reason,\n\t}\n\tbuf, _ := json.Marshal(body) // cannot fail\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, c.Endpoint+\"/v1/train\", strings.NewReader(string(buf)))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"content-type\", \"application/json\")\n\tfor k, v := range c.Headers {\n\t\treq.Header.Set(k, v)\n\t}\n\tclient := c.HTTPClient\n\tif client == nil {\n\t\tclient = http.DefaultClient\n\t}\n\tresp, err := client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\tif resp.StatusCode >= 300 {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"trainerclient: unexpected HTTP %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n\t}\n\tvar out struct {\n\t\tJobID string `json:\"job_id\"`\n\t}\n\tif err = json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&out); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif out.JobID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", errors.New(\"trainerclient: empty job_id\")\n\t}\n\treturn out.JobID, nil\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Concrete DriftHandler implementation\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// RetrainHandler is a DriftHandler that directly calls the trainer\n// service when drift occurs.\ntype RetrainHandler struct {\n\tclient TrainerClient\n}\n\n// NewRetrainHandler builds a handler with the supplied client.\nfunc NewRetrainHandler(client TrainerClient) *RetrainHandler {\n\treturn &RetrainHandler{client: client}\n}\n\n// OnDrift implements the DriftHandler interface.\nfunc (h *RetrainHandler) OnDrift(ctx context.Context, ev DriftEvent) error {\n\treason := fmt.Sprintf(\"psi=%.4f feature=%s at %s\", ev.PSI, ev.Feature, ev.Time.Format(time.RFC3339))\n\tjobID, err := h.client.TriggerRetrain(ctx, ev.ModelID, reason)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\"model\":  ev.ModelID,\n\t\t\"job_id\": jobID,\n\t}).Info(\"driftmonitor: auto-retraining kicked off\")\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Bootstrap helper for CLI or tests\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// RunDriftMonitorFromEnv is a convenience entry-point that wires the\n// monitor using environment variables. It is suitable for docker-ised\n// sidecars or local dev.\n//\n//   ECHOPULSE_BROKERS=broker1:9092,broker2:9092\n//   ECHOPULSE_MONITOR_TOPIC=feature_drift\n//   ECHOPULSE_GROUP_ID=ml_drift_monitor\n//   ECHOPULSE_PSI_THRESHOLD=0.2\n//\n// A Ctrl-C (SIGINT) will trigger graceful shutdown.\nfunc RunDriftMonitorFromEnv() error {\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Handle Ctrl-C.\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tsig := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\t\tsignal.Notify(sig, os.Interrupt)\n\t\t<-sig\n\t\tcancel()\n\t}()\n\n\tbrokers := strings.Split(os.Getenv(\"ECHOPULSE_BROKERS\"), \",\")\n\ttopic := getenvDefault(\"ECHOPULSE_MONITOR_TOPIC\", \"feature_drift\")\n\tgroup := getenvDefault(\"ECHOPULSE_GROUP_ID\", \"ml_drift_monitor\")\n\n\tpsiThreshold, _ := strconv.ParseFloat(getenvDefault(\"ECHOPULSE_PSI_THRESHOLD\", \"0.2\"), 64)\n\n\tcfg := Config{\n\t\tBrokers:      brokers,\n\t\tTopic:        topic,\n\t\tGroupID:      group,\n\t\tMinBytes:     1e4,           // 10KiB\n\t\tMaxBytes:     5e6,           // 5MiB\n\t\tMaxWait:      200 * time.Millisecond,\n\t\tPSIThreshold: psiThreshold,\n\t}\n\n\thandler := NewRetrainHandler(&HTTPTrainerClient{\n\t\tEndpoint: getenvDefault(\"ECHOPULSE_TRAINER_ENDPOINT\", \"http://trainer:8080\"),\n\t\tHeaders:  map[string]string{\"X-Requested-By\": \"echopulse-drift-monitor\"},\n\t})\n\n\tmonitor, err := NewDriftMonitor(ctx, cfg, handler)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tmonitor.Start()\n\t<-ctx.Done()\n\tmonitor.Stop()\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Utilities\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunc getenvDefault(key, def string) string {\n\tval := os.Getenv(key)\n\tif val == \"\" {\n\t\treturn def\n\t}\n\treturn val\n}\n\n// signal is imported lazily via side-effect for Unix platforms.\nvar signal struct {\n\tNotify func(chan<- os.Signal, ...os.Signal)\n}\n\n// init resolves the platform-specific signal package (stubbed for\n// Windows where syscall.SIGINT isn't defined).\nfunc init() {\n\tvar (\n\t\tonce sync.Once\n\t\terr  error\n\t)\n\tonce.Do(func() {\n\t\tsignal.Notify, err = loadSignalNotify()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t// If signal.Notify cannot be loaded the monitor still works,\n\t\t\t// but won't respond to Ctrl-C. Log and continue.\n\t\t\tlogrus.WithError(err).Warn(\"driftmonitor: signal Notify unavailable\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n\n// loadSignalNotify attempts to obtain the os/signal.Notify symbol\n// via reflection so the code compiles on all platforms without a\n// direct import (which would break on JS/WASM).\nfunc loadSignalNotify() (func(chan<- os.Signal, ...os.Signal), error) {\n\tpkg, err := importPkg(\"os/signal\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tsym, ok := pkg.Lookup(\"Notify\")\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"symbol Notify not found\")\n\t}\n\tfn, ok := sym.Interface().(func(chan<- os.Signal, ...os.Signal))\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"Notify has unexpected type\")\n\t}\n\treturn fn, nil\n}\n\n// importPkg loads a package by path at runtime. It relies on the\n// plugin mechanism which is unavailable on Windows/JS, but the code\n// remains safe thanks to graceful degradation.\nfunc importPkg(path string) (*plugin.Plugin, error) {\n\treturn nil, errors.New(\"dynamic import not supported in this build\")\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Mock helpers (used in unit-tests)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// NewTestHandler returns a no-op DriftHandler that records received\n// events into the supplied slice for assertions in tests.\nfunc NewTestHandler(out *[]DriftEvent) DriftHandler {\n\tvar mu sync.Mutex\n\treturn DriftHandlerFunc(func(_ context.Context, ev DriftEvent) error {\n\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\tdefer mu.Unlock()\n\t\t*out = append(*out, ev)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t})\n}\n\n// DriftHandlerFunc is a functional adapter to build handlers inline.\ntype DriftHandlerFunc func(ctx context.Context, ev DriftEvent) error\n\n// OnDrift implements the DriftHandler interface.\nfunc (f DriftHandlerFunc) OnDrift(ctx context.Context, ev DriftEvent) error { return f(ctx, ev) }\n```","numLines":421,"startLine":1,"totalLines":421}}}

56→\n    57→// ========================= Observer Pattern ==============================\n    58→\n    59→// Observer receives Predictions.\n    60→type Observer interface {\n    99→func (r *simpleRegistry) Resolve(ctx context.Context, name, version string) (Endpoint, error) {\n   100→\tr.mu.RLock()\n   101→\tdefer r.mu.RUnlock()\n   102→\tkey := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s:%s\", name, version)\n   103→\tep, ok := r.data[key]\n   104→\tif !ok {\n   105→\t\treturn Endpoint{}, errors.New(\"model not found in registry\")\n   106→\t}\n   107→\treturn ep, nil\n   108→}\n   109→\n   110→// ========================= Model Client =================================\n   111→\n   112→// ModelClient hides transport-level details and exposes a stateless Predict call.\n   113→type ModelClient interface {\n   114→\tPredict(ctx context.Context, text string) (float64, error)\n   115→\tClose() error\n   116→}\n   117→\n   118→type grpcModelClient struct {\n   119→\tcc       *grpc.ClientConn\n   120→\tmodel    string\n   121→\tlatency  time.Duration // artificial latency for the demo\n   122→\tfailRate float64       // chance to cause an error\n   123→}\n   124→\n   125→func newGRPCModelClient(model string, ep Endpoint) (*grpcModelClient, error) {\n   126→\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), ep.Timeout)\n   127→\tdefer cancel()\n   128→\tconn, err := grpc.DialContext(ctx, ep.HostPort, grpc.WithInsecure(), grpc.WithBlock())\n   129→\tif err != nil {\n   130→\t\treturn nil, err\n   131→\t}\n   132→\treturn &grpcModelClient{\n   133→\t\tcc:       conn,\n   134→\t\tmodel:    model,\n   135→\t\tlatency:  time.Millisecond * time.Duration(rand.Intn(20)+5),\n   136→\t\tfailRate: 0.05, // 5% simulated failure\n   137→\t}, nil\n   138→}\n   139→\n   140→func (c *grpcModelClient) Predict(ctx context.Context, text string) (float64, error) {\n   141→\tif rand.Float64() < c.failRate {\n   142→\t\treturn 0, errors.New(\"simulated inference failure\")\n   143→\t}\n   144→\tselect {\n   145→\tcase <-time.After(c.latency):\n   146→\t\t// Fake deterministic sentiment for the demo.\n   147→\t\thash := 0\n   148→\t\tfor _, r := range text {\n   149→\t\t\thash += int(r)\n   150→\t\t}\n   151→\t\tsentiment := (float64(hash%200) / 100.0) - 1.0 // −1 .. +1\n   152→\t\treturn sentiment, nil\n   153→\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n   154→\t\treturn 0, ctx.Err()\n   155→\t}\n   156→}\n   157→\n   158→func (c *grpcModelClient) Close() error { return c.cc.Close() }\n   159→\n   160→// ========================= Strategy Pattern =============================\n   161→\n   162→// PredictorStrategy chooses an implementation at runtime.\n   163→type PredictorStrategy interface {\n   164→\tPredict(ctx context.Context, ev SocialEvent) (Prediction, error)\n   165→}\n   166→\n   167→// highAccuracyStrategy may be slower but more accurate.\n   168→type highAccuracyStrategy struct {\n   169→\tclient ModelClient\n   170→\tmodel  string\n   171→}\n   172→\n   173→func (s *highAccuracyStrategy) Predict(ctx context.Context, ev SocialEvent) (Prediction, error) {\n   174→\tstart := time.Now()\n   175→\tval, err := s.client.Predict(ctx, ev.Text)\n   176→\tif err != nil {\n   177→\t\treturn Prediction{}, err\n   178→\t}\n   179→\treturn Prediction{\n   180→\t\tEventID:   ev.ID,\n   181→\t\tSentiment: val,\n   182→\t\tModelName: s.model,\n   183→\t\tLatency:   time.Since(start),\n   184→\t\tCreatedAt: time.Now(),\n   185→\t}, nil\n   186→}\n   187→\n   188→// lowLatencyStrategy returns a quick heuristic.\n   189→type lowLatencyStrategy struct {\n   190→\tmodel string\n   191→}\n   192→\n   193→func (s *lowLatencyStrategy) Predict(_ context.Context, ev SocialEvent) (Prediction, error) {\n   194→\t// Extremely naive bag-of-words approach for the demo.\n   195→\twords := 0\n   196→\tneg := 0\n   197→\tfor _, tok := range []byte(ev.Text) {\n   198→\t\tif tok == ' ' {\n   199→\t\t\twords++\n   200→\t\t}\n   201→\t\tif tok == '!' || tok == '?' || tok == ':' {\n   202→\t\t\tneg++\n   203→\t\t}\n   204→\t}\n   205→\tsentiment := 1.0 - float64(neg)/float64(words+1) // crude heuristic\n   206→\treturn Prediction{\n   207→\t\tEventID:   ev.ID,\n   208→\t\tSentiment: sentiment,\n   209→\t\tModelName: s.model,\n   210→\t\tLatency:   0,\n   211→\t\tCreatedAt: time.Now(),\n   212→\t}, nil\n   213→}\n   214→\n   215→// ========================= Router (Context Object) ======================\n   216→\n   217→// SentimentRouter routes events to an appropriate predictor then fans out\n   218→// results to observers.  It encapsulates the Strategy & Observer patterns.\n   219→type SentimentRouter struct {\n   220→\tworkers       int\n   221→\tregistry      ModelRegistry\n   222→\tobsMu         sync.RWMutex\n   223→\tobservers     []Observer\n   224→\teventCh       chan SocialEvent\n   225→\tctx           context.Context\n   226→\tcancel        context.CancelFunc\n   227→\twg            sync.WaitGroup\n   228→\taccClientOnce sync.Once\n   229→\taccClient     ModelClient // high-accuracy shared client\n   230→}\n   231→\n   232→func NewSentimentRouter(workers int, registry ModelRegistry) *SentimentRouter {\n   233→\tif workers <= 0 {\n   234→\t\tworkers = 4\n   235→\t}\n   236→\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n   237→\treturn &SentimentRouter{\n   238→\t\tworkers:  workers,\n   239→\t\tregistry: registry,\n   240→\t\teventCh:  make(chan SocialEvent, workers*2),\n   241→\t\tctx:      ctx,\n   242→\t\tcancel:   cancel,\n   243→\t}\n   244→}\n   245→\n   246→// Subscribe adds an observer that will receive Prediction notifications.\n   247→func (r *SentimentRouter) Subscribe(obs Observer) {\n   248→\tr.obsMu.Lock()\n   249→\tdefer r.obsMu.Unlock()\n   250→\tr.observers = append(r.observers, obs)\n   251→}\n   252→\n   253→// Push queues an event for asynchronous processing.\n   254→func (r *SentimentRouter) Push(ev SocialEvent) error {\n   255→\tselect {\n   256→\tcase r.eventCh <- ev:\n   257→\t\treturn nil\n   258→\tcase <-r.ctx.Done():\n   259→\t\treturn r.ctx.Err()\n   260→\t}\n   261→}\n   262→\n   263→// Start launches the worker pool.\n   264→func (r *SentimentRouter) Start() {\n   265→\tr.wg.Add(r.workers)\n   266→\tfor i := 0; i < r.workers; i++ {\n   267→\t\tgo r.worker(i)\n   268→\t}\n   269→}\n   270→\n   271→// Stop gracefully shuts down the router.\n   272→func (r *SentimentRouter) Stop() {\n   273→\tr.cancel()\n   274→\tr.wg.Wait()\n   275→\tif r.accClient != nil {\n   276→\t\t_ = r.accClient.Close()\n   277→\t}\n   278→\tclose(r.eventCh)\n   279→}\n   280→\n   281→// ========================= Internal Helpers =============================\n   282→\n   283→func (r *SentimentRouter) worker(id int) {\n   284→\tdefer r.wg.Done()\n   285→\tlog.Printf(\"[router] worker #%d online\", id)\n   286→\n   287→\tfor {\n   288→\t\tselect {\n   289→\t\tcase ev := <-r.eventCh:\n   290→\t\t\tr.processEvent(ev)\n   291→\t\tcase <-r.ctx.Done():\n   292→\t\t\treturn\n   293→\t\t}\n   294→\t}\n   295→}\n   296→\n   297→func (r *SentimentRouter) processEvent(ev SocialEvent) {\n   298→\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.ctx, 500*time.Millisecond)\n   299→\tdefer cancel()\n   300→\n   301→\tvar strat PredictorStrategy\n   302→\t// Simulated heuristics:\n   303→\t//   High-priority events -> high-accuracy model\n   304→\t//   Otherwise -> low-latency model\n   305→\tif ev.Priority > 5 {\n   306→\t\tclient, err := r.getAccurateClient(ctx)\n   307→\t\tif err != nil {\n   308→\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[router] resolve err (%s): %v; falling back\", ev.ID, err)\n   309→\t\t\tstrat = &lowLatencyStrategy{model: \"heuristic_v1\"}\n   310→\t\t} else {\n   311→\t\t\tstrat = &highAccuracyStrategy{\n   312→\t\t\t\tclient: client,\n   313→\t\t\t\tmodel:  \"bert-sentiment:10\",\n   314→\t\t\t}\n   315→\t\t}\n   316→\t} else {\n   317→\t\tstrat = &lowLatencyStrategy{model: \"heuristic_v1\"}\n   318→\t}\n   319→\n   320→\tpred, err := strat.Predict(ctx, ev)\n   321→\tif err != nil {\n   322→\t\tlog.Printf(\"[router] prediction failed (%s): %v\", ev.ID, err)\n   323→\t\treturn\n   324→\t}\n   325→\tr.notify(pred)\n   326→}\n   327→\n   328→func (r *SentimentRouter) getAccurateClient(ctx context.Context) (ModelClient, error) {\n   329→\tvar err error\n   330→\tr.accClientOnce.Do(func() {\n   331→\t\tep, e := r.registry.Resolve(ctx, \"bert-sentiment\", \"10\")\n   332→\t\tif e != nil {\n   333→\t\t\terr = e\n   334→\t\t\treturn\n   335→\t\t}\n   336→\t\t// If the model server isn't already running, spin up a local fake.\n   337→\t\tif ep.HostPort == \"inproc\" {\n   338→\t\t\tep.HostPort, e = launchInprocModelServer()\n   339→\t\t\tif e != nil {\n   340→\t\t\t\terr = e\n   341→\t\t\t\treturn\n   342→\t\t\t}\n   343→\t\t}\n   344→\t\tr.accClient, err = newGRPCModelClient(\"bert-sentiment:10\", ep)\n   345→\t})\n   346→\treturn r.accClient, err\n   347→}\n   348→\n   349→func (r *SentimentRouter) notify(p Prediction) {\n   350→\tr.obsMu.RLock()\n   351→\tdefer r.obsMu.RUnlock()\n   352→\tfor _, o := range r.observers {\n   353→\t\t// Fan-out in a separate goroutine to avoid blocking slow observers.\n   354→\t\tgo o.Notify(p)\n   355→\t}\n   356→}\n   357→\n   358→// ========================= Demo / Local Driver ==========================\n   359→\n   360→// launchInprocModelServer starts a dummy gRPC service on a random port.\n   361→// EchoPulse defers to dedicated model-serving clusters in prod; here we mock.\n   362→func launchInprocModelServer() (string, error) {\n   363→\tl, err := net.Listen(\"tcp\", \"127.0.0.1:0\") // random port\n   364→\tif err != nil {\n   365→\t\treturn \"\", err\n   366→\t}\n   367→\tgo func() {\n   368→\t\ts := grpc.NewServer()\n   369→\t\t// pb.RegisterInferenceServiceServer(s, &fakeServer{})\n   370→\t\tif err = s.Serve(l); err != nil {\n   371→\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[inproc-model] serve err: %v\", err)\n   372→\t\t}\n   373→\t}()\n   374→\treturn l.Addr().String(), nil\n   375→}\n   376→\n   377→// ========================= Usage Example ================================\n   378→\n   379→// This function is NOT executed automatically; appropriate predictor then fans out\n// results to observers.  It encapsulates the Strategy & Observer patterns.\ntype SentimentRouter struct {\n\tworkers       int\n\tregistry      ModelRegistry\n\tobsMu         sync.RWMutex\n\tobservers     []Observer\n\teventCh       chan SocialEvent\n\tctx           context.Context\n\tcancel        context.CancelFunc\n\twg            sync.WaitGroup\n\taccClientOnce sync.Once\n\taccClient     ModelClient // high-accuracy shared client\n}\n\nfunc NewSentimentRouter(workers int, registry ModelRegistry) *SentimentRouter {\n\tif workers <= 0 {\n\t\tworkers = 4\n\t}\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\treturn &SentimentRouter{\n\t\tworkers:  workers,\n\t\tregistry: registry,\n\t\teventCh:  make(chan SocialEvent, workers*2),\n\t\tctx:      ctx,\n\t\tcancel:   cancel,\n\t}\n}\n\n// Subscribe adds an observer that will receive Prediction notifications.\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) Subscribe(obs Observer) {\n\tr.obsMu.Lock()\n\tdefer r.obsMu.Unlock()\n\tr.observers = append(r.observers, obs)\n}\n\n// Push queues an event for asynchronous processing.\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) Push(ev SocialEvent) error {\n\tselect {\n\tcase r.eventCh <- ev:\n\t\treturn nil\n\tcase <-r.ctx.Done():\n\t\treturn r.ctx.Err()\n\t}\n}\n\n// Start launches the worker pool.\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) Start() {\n\tr.wg.Add(r.workers)\n\tfor i := 0; i < r.workers; i++ {\n\t\tgo r.worker(i)\n\t}\n}\n\n// Stop gracefully shuts down the router.\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) Stop() {\n\tr.cancel()\n\tr.wg.Wait()\n\tif r.accClient != nil {\n\t\t_ = r.accClient.Close()\n\t}\n\tclose(r.eventCh)\n}\n\n// ========================= Internal Helpers =============================\n\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) worker(id int) {\n\tdefer r.wg.Done()\n\tlog.Printf(\"[router] worker #%d online\", id)\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase ev := <-r.eventCh:\n\t\t\tr.processEvent(ev)\n\t\tcase <-r.ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) processEvent(ev SocialEvent) {\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.ctx, 500*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tvar strat PredictorStrategy\n\t// Simulated heuristics:\n\t//   High-priority events -> high-accuracy model\n\t//   Otherwise -> low-latency model\n\tif ev.Priority > 5 {\n\t\tclient, err := r.getAccurateClient(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[router] resolve err (%s): %v; falling back\", ev.ID, err)\n\t\t\tstrat = &lowLatencyStrategy{model: \"heuristic_v1\"}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tstrat = &highAccuracyStrategy{\n\t\t\t\tclient: client,\n\t\t\t\tmodel:  \"bert-sentiment:10\",\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tstrat = &lowLatencyStrategy{model: \"heuristic_v1\"}\n\t}\n\n\tpred, err := strat.Predict(ctx, ev)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[router] prediction failed (%s): %v\", ev.ID, err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tr.notify(pred)\n}\n\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) getAccurateClient(ctx context.Context) (ModelClient, error) {\n\tvar err error\n\tr.accClientOnce.Do(func() {\n\t\tep, e := r.registry.Resolve(ctx, \"bert-sentiment\", \"10\")\n\t\tif e != nil {\n\t\t\terr = e\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\t// If the model server isn't already running, spin up a local fake.\n\t\tif ep.HostPort == \"inproc\" {\n\t\t\tep.HostPort, e = launchInprocModelServer()\n\t\t\tif e != nil {\n\t\t\t\terr = e\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tr.accClient, err = newGRPCModelClient(\"bert-sentiment:10\", ep)\n\t})\n\treturn r.accClient, err\n}\n\nfunc (r *SentimentRouter) notify(p Prediction) {\n\tr.obsMu.RLock()\n\tdefer r.obsMu.RUnlock()\n\tfor _, o := range r.observers {\n\t\t// Fan-out in a separate goroutine to avoid blocking slow observers.\n\t\tgo o.Notify(p)\n\t}\n}\n\n// ========================= Demo / Local Driver ==========================\n\n// launchInprocModelServer starts a dummy gRPC service on a random port.\n// EchoPulse defers to dedicated model-serving clusters in prod; here we mock.\nfunc launchInprocModelServer() (string, error) {\n\tl, err := net.Listen(\"tcp\", \"127.0.0.1:0\") // random port\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\ts := grpc.NewServer()\n\t\t// pb.RegisterInferenceServiceServer(s, &fakeServer{})\n\t\tif err = s.Serve(l); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[inproc-model] serve err: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\treturn l.Addr().String(), nil\n}\n\n// ========================= Usage Example ================================\n\n// This function is NOT executed automatically; this would live in a shared protobuf/avro schema.\ntype SocialEvent struct {\n\tChannelID   string    `json:\"channel_id\"`\n\tUserID      string    `json:\"user_id\"`\n\tText        string    `json:\"text\"`\n\tSentiment   float64   `json:\"sentiment\"` // Pre-computed sentiment score (-1..1)\n\tCreatedAt   time.Time `json:\"created_at\"`\n\tEventID     string    `json:\"event_id\"`\n\tContentType string    `json:\"content_type\"` // text/emoji/audio/…\n}\n\n// CommunityHealthEvent is a higher-level signal generated by this module and\n// fanned-out to downstream services/dashboards.\ntype CommunityHealthEvent struct {\n\tChannelID string    `json:\"channel_id\"`\n\tScore     float64   `json:\"score\"` // EWMA sentiment score.\n\tWindow    string    `json:\"window\"`\n\tCreatedAt time.Time `json:\"created_at\"`\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Configuration\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Config is resolved from environment variables so the binary can be\n// containerised and scheduled without additional configuration management.\ntype Config struct {\n\tBrokers        []string\n\tIncomingTopic  string\n\tOutgoingTopic  string\n\tConsumerGroup  string\n\tAlpha          float64 // Smoothing factor for EWMA.\n\tMetricsAddress string  // e.g. \":2112\"\n}\n\n// NewConfigFromEnv reads & validates configuration from environment variables.\nfunc NewConfigFromEnv() (Config, error) {\n\tcfg := Config{\n\t\tBrokers:        strings.Split(os.Getenv(\"EP_BROKERS\"), \",\"),\n\t\tIncomingTopic:  getenvDefault(\"EP_IN_TOPIC\", \"social.events\"),\n\t\tOutgoingTopic:  getenvDefault(\"EP_OUT_TOPIC\", \"community.health\"),\n\t\tConsumerGroup:  getenvDefault(\"EP_GROUP_ID\", \"sentiment-aggregator\"),\n\t\tAlpha:          getenvFloatDefault(\"EP_ALPHA\", 0.3),\n\t\tMetricsAddress: getenvDefault(\"EP_METRICS_ADDR\", \":2112\"),\n\t}\n\n\tif len(cfg.Brokers) == 0 || cfg.Brokers[0] == \"\" {\n\t\treturn Config{}, errors.New(\"EP_BROKERS cannot be empty\")\n\t}\n\tif cfg.Alpha <= 0.0 || cfg.Alpha >= 1.0 {\n\t\treturn Config{}, errors.New(\"EP_ALPHA must be in (0,1)\")\n\t}\n\treturn cfg, nil\n}\n\n// Helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunc getenvDefault(key, def string) string {\n\tif v := strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv(key)); v != \"\" {\n\t\treturn v\n\t}\n\treturn def\n}\n\nfunc getenvFloatDefault(key string, def float64) float64 {\n\tif v := strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv(key)); v != \"\" {\n\t\tvar f float64\n\t\t_, err := fmt.Sscan(v, &f)\n\t\tif err == nil {\n\t\t\treturn f\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn def\n}\n\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Sentiment Aggregator\n// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// SentimentAggregator consumes raw SocialEvents, maintains a per-channel\n// exponential moving average of sentiment, and publishes CommunityHealthEvents.\ntype SentimentAggregator struct {\n\tcfg      Config\n\tproducer sarama.SyncProducer\n\tconsumer sarama.ConsumerGroup\n\n\t// state protects the rolling score for backoff.NewExponentialBackOff()\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\t// Consume() is blocking, returning only on error or ctx cancel.\n\t\t\tif err := sa.consumer.Consume(ctx, []string{sa.cfg.IncomingTopic}, &consumerGroupHandler{agg: sa}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Transient errors (e.g. broker down) are back-off retried.\n\t\t\t\tif ctx.Err() != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t// context cancelled -> graceful shutdown\n\t\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tnext := backoffCfg.NextBackOff()\n\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"consume error: %v; retrying in %s\", err, next)\n\t\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\t\tcase <-time.After(next):\n\t\t\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Reached here if Consume exited without error -> context cancelled.\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\t<-ctx.Done() // Wait for caller.\n\twg.Wait()\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Stop gracefully closes the Kafka clients.\nfunc (sa *SentimentAggregator) Stop() {\n\t_ = sa.consumer.Close()\n\t_ = sa.producer.Close()\n}\n\n// processSocialEvent updates EWMA state and publishes a CommunityHealthEvent.\nfunc (sa *SentimentAggregator) processSocialEvent(ev *SocialEvent) error {\n\tsa.metricMessages.Inc()\n\n\tsa.stateMu.Lock()\n\tst, ok := sa.state[ev.ChannelID]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\tst = &channelState{}\n\t\tsa.state[ev.ChannelID] = st\n\t}\n\t// EWMA update: S_t = α*x_t + (1-α)*S_{t-1}\n\tst.Score = sa.cfg.Alpha*ev.Sentiment + 76→// statistical drift is detected with significance < alpha.\n    77→type DriftMonitor struct {\n    78→\tcfg          DriftMonitorConfig\n    79→\tbus          BusConsumer\n    80→\tfs 237→\t// Fan-out workers\n   238→\tfor i := 0; i < a.cfg.WorkerConcurrency; i++ {\n   239→\t\twg.Add(1)\n   240→\t\tgo func(id int) {\n   241→\t\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n   242→\t\t\ta.workerLoop(ctx,  263→\t\tselect {\n   264→\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n   265→\t\t\treturn\n   266→\t\tdefault:\n   267→\t\t}\n   268→\n   269→\t\tmsg, err := a.reader.FetchMessage(ctx)\n   270→\t\tif err != nil {\n   271→\t\t\tif errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {\n   272→\t\t\t\treturn\n   273→\t\t\t}\n   274→\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[worker %d] fetch error: %v\", workerID, err)\n   275→\t\t\tcontinue\n   276→\t\t}\n   277→\n   278→\t\tstartAggregate := time.Now()\n   279→\t\tvar se SocialEvent\n   280→\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Value, &se); err != nil {\n   281→\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[worker %d] unmarshal error: 287→\t\tif err != nil {\n   288→\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[worker %d] scoring error: %v\", workerID, err)\n   289→\t\t\t_ = a.reader.CommitMessages(ctx, msg) // swallow error\n   290→\t\t\tcontinue\n 296→\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[worker %d] publish error: %v\", workerID, err)\n   297→\t\t\t// do not commit so we can retry\n   298→\t\t\tcontinue\n   299→\t\t}\n   300→\n   301→\t\tif err := a.reader.CommitMessages(ctx, msg); err != nil {\n   302→\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[worker %d] commit error: %v\", workerID, err)\n   303→\t\t}\n   304→\t\tprocessedEvents.Inc()\n   305→\t}\n   306→}\n   307→\n   308→func (a *Aggregator) scoreEvent(ctx context.Context, se SocialEvent) (HealthScoreEvent, error) {\n   309→\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n   310→\tscoreMap := sync.Map{}\n   311→\terrChan := 317→\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n   318→\t\t\tscore, err := s.Score(ctx, se)\n   319→\t\t\tif err != nil {\n   320→\t\t\t\terrChan <- fmt.Errorf(\"%s: %w\", s.Name(), err)\n   321→\t\t\t\treturn\n 328→\twg.Wait()\n   329→\tclose(errChan)\n   330→\tfor err := range errChan {\n   331→\t\treturn HealthScoreEvent{}, err\n   332→\t}\n   333→\n   334→\tcombined := 0.0\n   335→\tscoreMap.Range(func(key, value interface{}) bool {\n   336→\t\tcombined += value.(float64)\n   337→\t\treturn true\n   338→\t})\n   339→\tcombined = combined / float64(len(a.strategies)) // interface{}) bool {\n   346→\t\tm[k.(string)] = v.(float64)\n   347→\t\treturn true\n   348→\t})\n   349→\n   350→\treturn HealthScoreEvent{\n   351→\t\tSocialEventID:  se.ID,\n   352→\t\tScore:          combined,\n   353→\t\tStrategyScores: m,\n   354→\t\tGeneratedAt:    time.Now(),\n   355→\t}, nil\n   356→}\n   357→\n   358→func (a *Aggregator) publishHealth(ctx context.Context, h -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   298→\n   299→// tokenAggregator maintains an exponentially decayed score per token.\n   300→//\n   301→//\t\tscore(t) = score(t-1) * 0.5^(Δ / halfLife) + 1\n   302→//\n   303→// where Δ is time elapsed since the last update.\n   304→type tokenAggregator struct {\n   305→\tmu       sync.RWMutex\n   306→\thalfLife 334→\t\t}\n   335→\n   336→\t\tdecay := mathPowHalf(float64(now.Sub(ent.last)) / float64(ta.halfLife))\n   337→\t\tent.score = ent.score*decay + 1\n   338→\t\tent.last = now\n   339→\t}\n   340→}\n   341→\n   342→func (ta *tokenAggregator) topK(k int) []TrendingToken {\n   343→\tta.mu.RLock()\n   344→\tdefer ta.mu.RUnlock()\n   345→\n   346→\t// Guard against empty map.\n   347→\tif ta.entries {\n   354→\t\t// Apply decay so old tokens don’t artificially persist.\n   355→\t\tdecay := mathPowHalf(float64(time.Since(ent.last)) / float64(ta.halfLife))\n   356→\t\tscore := ent.score 363→\tsort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Score > out[j].Score })\n   364→\n   365→\tif k > len(out) {\n   366→\t\tk = len(out)\n   367→\t}\n   368→\treturn out[:k]\n   369→}\n   370→\n   371→// mathPowHalf computes 0.5^(x)\n   372→// It’s faster than math.Pow for tiny bases, avoids import of math pkg.\n   373→func mathPowHalf(x float64) float64 {\n   374→\t// log2(e) ≈ 1.4426950408889634, 0.5^x = e^(x * ln(0.5))\n   375→\tconst lnHalf = -0.6931471805599453\n   376→\treturn exp(x * lnHalf)\n   377→}\n   378→\n   379→// exp is a naive approximation of e^x using the first 10 terms of the\n   380→// Taylor expansion.  Replace with math.Exp in production builds to avoid\n   381→// precision loss.  Here we re-implement to remove heavy math dependency.\n   382→func exp(x float64) float64 {\n   383→\tsum, term := 1.0, 1.0\n   384→\tfor i := 1; i < 10; i++ {\n   385→\t\tterm *= x / float64(i)\n   386→\t\tsum += term\n   387→\t}\n   388→\treturn sum\n   389→}\n   390→\n   391→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   392→// Tokenization Helpers\n   393→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   394→\n   395→// tokenize splits text into lower-cased tokens, removes punctuation and stop\n   396→// words, and filters short tokens.\n   397→func tokenize(s string, minLen int) []string {\n   398→\ts = strings.ToLower(s)\n   399→\t// Replace punctuation with spaces.\n   400→\ts = strings.Map(func(r rune) rune {\n   401→\t\tif r >= 'a' && r <= 'z' || r >= '0' && r <= '9' || r == ' ' {\n   402→\t\t\treturn r\n   403→\t\t}\n   404→\t\treturn ' '\n   405→\t}, s)\n   406→\n   407→\tfields := strings.Fields(s)\n   408→\tout := make([]string, 0, len(fields))\n   409→\tfor _, f := range fields {\n   410→\t\tif len(f) < minLen {\n   411→\t\t\tcontinue\n   412→\t\t}\n   413→\t\tif _, stop := stopWords[f]; stop {\n   414→\t\t\tcontinue\n   415→\t\t}\n   416→\t\tout = append(out, f)\n   417→\t}\n   418→\treturn out\n   419→}\n   420→\n   421→// A (very small) built-in English stop-word list.
